**Panel 5** — Sophia beside him, quieter, watching with mixed wonder and unease.
> "It's beautiful… and scary."

**Panel 6** — Thuraya standing calm and steady, watching the wadi with practiced ease, no fear in her posture.
> "When the wadi comes like this, it means good rain in the mountains."

**Panel 7** — Thuraya's face shifting into a small knowing smile, a plan forming.
> "It also means… we can go to Tawi Zahir."

**Panel 8** — All four children's faces lighting up at once in a burst of shared excitement.
> The children's eyes lit up, all at the same time.

**Panel 9** — Four-way split reactions: Mansoor fist-pumping "Swimming!", Noura clapping "Picnic!", Khalil cheering "No school!", Ahmed frowning "Careful!"
> Four children, four instant reactions.

**Panel 10** — John scratching his head, puzzled, looking at Thuraya.
> "What is Tawi Zahir?"

**Panel 11** — Thuraya gesturing patiently, describing clear pools with her hands, John and Sophia listening.
> "Pools. Clear water. People go to swim, wash, relax."

**Panel 12** — Sophia clapping with excitement, John grinning beside her, Malood the goat darting between their legs in the background as thunder booms again.
> Sophia: "A trip!" John: "A trip!"

### Scene 5 — In the Classroom — Past Continuous

**Panel 1** — Sophia walking back toward the school under a suddenly clean, deep-blue sky, pausing to look up.
> The rain stopped as suddenly as it began.

**Panel 2** — Wide shot of the washed mountains and village, unusually sharp and vivid, dust gone from the air.
> Everything looks freshly washed.

**Panel 3** — Inside the classroom: students at their desks, muddy feet, restless energy, still buzzing from the morning.
> The rain's energy hasn't drained from the room yet.

**Panel 4** — John at the chalkboard writing "WEATHER" and a list of words ending carefully with "wadi."
> John adds "wadi" to the board, with ceremony.

**Panel 5** — John turning to face the class, Sophia standing beside him adding her own line.
> John: "Today, we talk about the weather." Sophia: "And about what we were doing."

**Panel 6** — John writing "Past Continuous" grammar pattern on the board, then pointing directly at Mansoor.
> "Mansoor. What were you doing when it started raining?"

**Panel 7** — Mansoor standing up proudly at his desk, chest out.
> "I was… jumping."

**Panel 8** — Noura pausing in thought, finger to her chin, then answering.
> "I was… running."

**Panel 9** — Sophia laughing at herself in front of the class, students grinning back at her.
> "I was putting bowls under the roof."

**Panel 10** — John pointing to himself with mock seriousness, class laughing harder.
> "I was sleeping."

**Panel 11** — Ali raising his hand and pointing out the window; the whole class rushing to the window to see a rainbow forming over the village.
> "Teacher… the rainbow?"

**Panel 12** — Quiet moment: Saeed at the back of the room raising his hand slowly; Thuraya watching him with soft, attentive eyes.
> Saeed: "When it started raining, I was… watching."

### Scene 6 — Planning the Trip — The Village After Rain

**Panel 1** — After-school gathering outside the school; the wadi still visibly running loud and strong in the background.
> The wadi is still running, loud and strong.

**Panel 2** — John and Thuraya standing together watching the wadi.
> John: "Tawi Zahir must be amazing." Thuraya: "We should go tomorrow."

**Panel 3** — Sophia clapping with excitement beside them.
> "Yes! A real Omani picnic!"

**Panel 4** — The group approaching Uncle Nasser, who stands leaning against his pickup truck, calmly drinking tea.
> They go to see Uncle Nasser.

**Panel 5** — Nasser nodding, gesturing toward his truck; Mansoor jumping with excitement beside him.
> Nasser: "Tomorrow? Good. I will take you." Mansoor: "With the pickup?"

**Panel 6** — Wide village scene: neighbors, aunts, and children gathering informally, conversations overlapping as the trip plan spreads.
> Who brings food? Who brings water? Who brings firewood?

**Panel 7** — Uncle Marhoon standing proudly, announcing meat for the trip; John's eyes wide; Malood the goat trotting after Marhoon.
> Marhoon: "I will bring meat. Meshkak." John: "Meat?"

**Panel 8** — Evening wide shot of the village: everything green and clean, mountains sharp and close, birds visible mid-flight.
> That evening, Samail was different — green, clean, fresh.

**Panel 9** — Sophia standing outside the house at dusk, breathing deeply, John beside her looking at the mountains.
> Sophia: "The most beautiful day since we came." John: "Oman is showing us its secret."

**Panel 10** — The group looking once more at the still-running wadi in the fading light, Thuraya speaking quietly.
> "Tomorrow… Tawi Zahir."

**Panel 11** — Mansoor shouting from down the lane; John shouting back with a grin; Sophia laughing beside him.
> Mansoor: "I am bringing my swimming clothes!" John: "I am bringing… courage!"

**Panel 12** — Malood the goat slipping in the mud, standing back up unfazed, and calmly eating a leaf as the village settles into evening quiet.
> Malood, unbothered, eats a leaf.


---


## Palm Twenty-Three — Tawi Zahir — The Pools, the Picnic, and the Mountain Cousin

### Scene 1 — Before the Sun Finished Waking

**Panel 1** — Pre-dawn village lane, still deep blue at the edges; Uncle Nasser's pickup truck headlights cut into the dark, engine visibly rattling.
> "A village that wakes faster than its sun."

**Panel 2** — Close on the truck's old engine/hood, Uncle Nasser at the wheel looking proud despite the rough sound.
> "Engines that argued were engines that were thinking."

**Panel 3** — John and Sophia standing in the open truck bed, gripping the side rail carefully, unsure of the protocol, dawn light behind them.
> Two foreigners riding in a pickup bed for the first time.

**Panel 4** — Sophia leaning toward John, whispering dramatically, hand near her mouth.
> "If I fall, please tell my mother I tried."

**Panel 5** — John, standing firm beside her, jaw set bravely.
> "If you fall, I fall with you."

**Panel 6** — Ms. Thuraya seated calmly in the front cab, composed, watching the road.
> The passenger-seat composure of a well-traveled teacher.

**Panel 7** — The village children piling into the truck bed behind John and Sophia, loud, half-dressed, buzzing with energy.
> A night's worth of anticipation, barely slept off.

**Panel 8** — Mansoor bouncing in place, unable to stay still, fists in the air.
> "We are going! We are going! We are really going!"

**Panel 9** — Ahmed crouched over the picnic bags, counting items seriously.
> "Food is here. Water is here. Plates are here. Good."

**Panel 10** — Khalil leaning forward with a sly, scheming grin, scanning the group.
> Already planning trouble, as always.

**Panel 11** — Malood the goat squeezed in among the children and bags, entirely at home.
> Where the village went, Malood went.

### Scene 2 — A Place That Was a Dream

**Panel 1** — The pickup climbing a narrow, dusty brown mountain road, curves tightening as it rises.
> A road that hadn't decided what it would show you yet.

**Panel 2** — Sudden shift in the same road: brown rock gives way to a burst of green trees and wildflowers, a running falaj visible.
> The brown gave way and the green arrived — full and shocking.

**Panel 3** — Wide establishing shot of Tawi Zahir: clear pools among rocks, heavy green palms, birds overhead; the whole group standing silent in awe.
> When they reached Tawi Zahir, everyone became quiet.

**Panel 4** — Close-up on a pool's surface, water so clear the brown stones are visible on the bottom.
> Pools that existed only in the brief window after rain.

**Panel 5** — Sophia standing at the pool's edge, hand covering her mouth in wonder.
> "This is… a dream."

**Panel 6** — John beside her, staring at the water, calculating its coldness with a wary expression.
> "This is… cold."

**Panel 7** — Mansoor already barefoot, tossing his shoes aside in one motion.
> Feet in the water within ten seconds — no obstacle allowed.

**Panel 8** — Ali, Noura, and Khalil mid-shout, each with a different reaction, near the pool's edge.
> "Swimming!" — "Wait!" — "Last one is a chicken!"

**Panel 9** — All the children sprinting toward the pools at once, a chaotic blur of motion across the rocks.
> Suddenly everyone was running.

**Panel 10** — Thuraya standing behind them clapping her hands, calling out, entirely ignored.
> "Careful! Don't run on the rocks!" Nobody listened.

### Scene 3 — Setting Up the Picnic

**Panel 1** — Wide shot of the picnic site filling gradually: mats unrolled, coolers set down, families arriving on foot and by truck, qafeer baskets stacked.
> The unspoken protocol of the village potluck.

**Panel 2** — Uncle Marhoon arriving proudly, holding up a bag of meat.
> "Meshkak," he said proudly.

**Panel 3** — John's face lighting up instantly at the sight of the meat bag.
> The word meshkak always meant joy was coming.

**Panel 4** — Sophia and Thuraya crouched together, arranging three flat stones into a cooking hearth.
> An ancient hearth-design needing no instruction manual.

**Panel 5** — Thuraya lighting the fire with quick, practiced wrist movements; Sophia watching closely.
> A thousand fires started, a thousand more to come.

**Panel 6** — Sophia cutting vegetables with careful, focused concentration.
> Precise vegetable-cutting, Northern-style.

**Panel 7** — Thuraya washing rice, both hands rotating it in a metal bowl, cloudy water being poured off.
> A ritual her hands no longer needed to think about.

**Panel 8** — John strolling around the cooking area with his hands on his hips like a supervisor; Thuraya giving him a flat, unimpressed look.
> "I am… supervising." / "You are in the way."

**Panel 9** — Malood nosing his way into the open food bag while no one is looking.
> Malood moved too — into the food bag.

**Panel 10** — Sophia shouting and lunging as Malood bolts away with a mouthful of green leaves.
> "NO!"

**Panel 11** — John chasing Malood across the site, children doubled over laughing at the scene.
> "Come back here, criminal!"

### Scene 4 — In the Water

**Panel 1** — Wide shot of the pool: Ali swimming smoothly, Mansoor mid-jump off a rock, Khalil splashing everyone nearby, Ahmed sitting cautiously at the edge touching the water.
> Each boy had found his own kind of water.

**Panel 2** — Close on Ahmed dipping a hand in, wincing; Mansoor mid-air behind him.
> "It's cold." / "Yes. That's the point."

**Panel 3** — Sequence panel of John's foot dipping in and out of the water repeatedly at the pool's edge.
> The diplomatic foot-in-foot-out of a negotiating man.

**Panel 4** — Sophia on the bank, arms crossed, laughing at John's hesitation.
> "You are not swimming. You are negotiating."

**Panel 5** — John leaping into the pool, mid-air, committing fully.
> Pride's calculations finally complete.

**Panel 6** — John's face frozen in shock as he surfaces, mouth open, screaming from the cold.
> A body-temperature recalibrated in one second.

**Panel 7** — Everyone around the pool clapping and cheering for him.
> Everyone clapped.

**Panel 8** — John standing chest-deep in the water, arms raised triumphantly.
> "I am alive!"

### Scene 5 — The Lesson Without a Blackboard

**Panel 1** — Thuraya and Sophia exchanging a knowing, conspiratorial smile by the water's edge.
> Two teachers spotting the same teachable moment.

**Panel 2** — Sophia standing and calling out to the children, who groan but start gathering around her.
> "Okay! English time!"

**Panel 3** — John standing next to Sophia, speaking slowly and clearly to the gathered children.
> "Today… we use Present Continuous."

**Panel 4** — John pointing at Ali, still in the water, who answers calmly.
> "Ali, what are you doing?" / "I am swimming."

**Panel 5** — John pointing at Mansoor, mid-jump again, shouting his answer in the air.
> "I am jumping!"

**Panel 6** — John pointing at Sophia, who holds up her wooden spoon proudly; Thuraya beside her.
> "I am cooking." / "I am washing."

**Panel 7** — The group turning to look at Uncle Marhoon at his fire, carefully turning skewers of meshkak.
> "He is cooking meat!" "He is making meshkak!"

**Panel 8** — John crouched, writing the grammar structure in the sand with a stick, children watching.
> I am… / He is… / They are… + verb-ing

**Panel 9** — Khalil raising his hand, grinning, pointing toward Malood in the background.
> "Teacher… Malood?"

**Panel 10** — Everyone turning to see Malood caught eating leaves from a reclaimed food bag.
> Caught again, mid-crime.

**Panel 11** — John sighing as the children chant back the sentence in unison.
> "Malood is always eating."

### Scene 6 — Disasters, Afternoon, and the Quiet of Saeed

**Panel 1** — Sophia stirring a cooking pot, tasting a spoonful, her face suddenly changing in alarm.
> Her face changed.

**Panel 2** — Sophia running toward the water, fanning her open mouth.
> "HOT! HOT! VERY HOT!"

**Panel 3** — Thuraya tasting the same dish and laughing at Sophia's reaction.
> "You put too much chili."

**Panel 4** — John tasting it too, going silent, standing with mouth open facing into the breeze.
> No words — just an open mouth and the wind.

**Panel 5** — A line of John, Sophia, and three children all standing with mouths open facing the air, Mansoor pointing at them, laughing.
> "You look like fish."

**Panel 6** — Uncle Marhoon proudly presenting the finished meshkak; John eating skewer after skewer while Sophia watches, amused.
> "Slowly." / "I am enjoying Oman."

**Panel 7** — Malood snatching a skewer of meshkak and dashing off as John shouts after him.
> "That goat is impossible!"

**Panel 8** — Wide, relaxed afternoon shot: people lounging on mats, some swimming, some sleeping, some eating, fire smoking gently, children scattered practicing sentences.
> "I am swimming!" "He is sleeping!" "They are playing!"

**Panel 9** — Saeed sitting alone on a high rock overlooking the whole scene, quiet and watchful.
> The boy who missed nothing from the edge.

**Panel 10** — Thuraya climbing up to sit near him, asking gently; Saeed smiling shyly.
> "What are you doing?" / "I am… thinking."

**Panel 11** — Sunset light over the wadi; the group slowly packing mats, coolers, and baskets, tired and content.
> Everyone was tired and happy.

**Panel 12** — John and Sophia taking one last look at the pools before climbing into the truck; Malood already asleep inside a bag.
> "This is… one of the best days of my life." / "Mine too."


---


## Palm Twenty-Four — The Camera, the Tools, and the Fastest Goat in Samail

### Scene 1 — Something Special

**Panel 1** — Village classroom, morning. John stands near the blackboard holding a bag, his face very serious and theatrical. Twelve children lean forward in their seats, sensing something unusual.
> "Today… we have… something special."

**Panel 2** — Close on the back row: Sophia sits beside Thuraya, smiling knowingly, clearly in on the surprise John is about to reveal.
> Sophia watched him with a small wifely smile, already knowing what was coming.

**Panel 3** — Three boys in the back row whisper to each other, each with a different guess written on his face — one hopeful, one curious, one just excited.
> Mansoor: "Maybe sweets." Ahmed: "Maybe books." Khalil: "Maybe trouble."

**Panel 4** — John carefully opens the bag, children craning their necks to see inside.
> John opened the bag carefully.

**Panel 5** — John lifts out a black leather-strapped camera, holding it up like a treasure. The children's eyes go wide.
> He took out a black camera.

**Panel 6** — Chaos erupts — kids half out of their seats, pointing, shouting over each other.
> "Abooooy!" "What is that?" "Does it bite?" "Is it a radio?"

**Panel 7** — Sophia laughing, gesturing to calm them, explaining simply.
> "No, no. This is a camera."

**Panel 8** — Ali raises his hand with a serious, slightly worried expression; John blinks in surprise at the question.
> "Teacher… it steals faces?"

**Panel 9** — John raises the camera to his eye, aiming it at the class, finger on the shutter.
> "No. It… keeps memories."

**Panel 10** — Flash/motion lines around the camera as it clicks; half the class visibly jumps in their seats, startled.
> CLICK. Half the class jumped.

**Panel 11** — Mansoor clutching his chest dramatically as if shot; Thuraya laughing beside him, reassuring.
> Mansoor: "It shot me!" Thuraya: "It's not a gun. It's a camera."


### Scene 2 — We Use … For …

**Panel 1** — John at the blackboard writing "We use … for …" in chalk, class repeating aloud in unison.
> "Repeat: We use…" — The class: "We use…"

**Panel 2** — John points to the camera on his desk, prompting the class with a questioning look.
> "We use a camera for…?"

**Panel 3** — Ahmed sits thinking hard, one finger to his chin, before answering confidently.
> "For… taking pictures."

**Panel 4** — Thuraya stands and carries in a small woven palm-leaf basket, setting it on the table in front of the class.
> Thuraya brought a small basket of ordinary village tools.

**Panel 5** — Thuraya lifts out a ma'wal (hoe) and holds it up; Ali answers proudly, hand raised.
> "We use a ma'wal for…?" — Ali: "For farming."

**Panel 6** — Thuraya holds up a heeb (small sickle); Noura answers from her seat.
> "And this?" — Noura: "For cutting grass."

**Panel 7** — Thuraya holds up the majz; several children laugh nervously, a couple shrinking back slightly.
> Some students laughed nervously at the majz.

**Panel 8** — Thuraya speaking carefully to the class, choosing her words; John nods slowly beside her.
> "We use a majz for… cutting and… treatment." — John: "Oman has… very brave medicine."

**Panel 9** — Sophia presses a mowqa'a (garlic crusher); the class bursts out laughing.
> "We use this for garlic!"

**Panel 10** — Thuraya holds up a small silver kohl stick, gesturing near her eye.
> "We use this for… eyes."

**Panel 11** — The blackboard now full of chalk sentences, John standing back admiring it happily, hands on hips.
> "This is… a perfect lesson."

**Panel 12** — Off in the corner, unnoticed by the adults, Malood the goat's snout inches toward the camera on the table.
> That was the moment Malood chose to become famous.


### Scene 3 — The Fastest Goat in Samail

**Panel 1** — Close-up: the camera is gone from John's hand — it is now clamped in Malood the goat's mouth, strap dangling.
> Suddenly — the camera was in Malood's mouth.

**Panel 2** — Malood turns sharply, ears back, eyes alert, about to bolt.
> Malood turned. Malood ran.

**Panel 3** — Sophia and John both lunge forward, mouths open, shouting, pointing at the fleeing goat.
> Sophia: "THE CAMERA!" John: "STOP THAT GOAT!"

**Panel 4** — Wide shot of the classroom in chaos — chairs knocked aside, Mansoor, Khalil, and Ali sprinting after the goat.
> The class exploded into chaos.

**Panel 5** — Malood leaping over a school bag and ducking under a table, camera still in his mouth, heading for the door.
> He jumped over a bag. He passed under a table. He escaped through the door.

**Panel 6** — Outside in the dusty yard, Malood dashes in tight zigzag circles, evading the chasing children.
> In the yard, Malood ran in circles — like a champion.

**Panel 7** — A chase line strung across the yard: John (holding his bag) running, then Sophia, then Thuraya, then Ahmed clutching his notebook even mid-run.
> John ran after him. Sophia ran after John. Thuraya ran after Sophia. Ahmed ran after everyone — still holding a notebook.

**Panel 8** — Sadou the shop owner appears at the yard's edge, a box in his arms, watching the goat barrel toward him with instant understanding on his face.
> A hero appeared: Sadou the shop owner.

**Panel 9** — Sadou sets down his box and opens his arms wide as Malood charges straight at him.
> He put the box down. He opened his arms. Malood ran straight into him.

**Panel 10** — Sadou catches Malood mid-air like a football, staggering back triumphantly; onlooking children and teachers applaud from behind.
> Sadou caught him like a football. The yard clapped.

**Panel 11** — John arrives out of breath, takes the camera back from the goat's mouth, and hugs it to his chest with relief.
> "Thank you, Sadou. You saved my memories."

**Panel 12** — Sadou grinning proudly, one hand still on Malood's back, delivering his line to the laughing class.
> "Malood always want be star."


### Scene 4 — Many Pictures

**Panel 1** — John moving through the schoolyard with the camera raised, snapping photos of students at play.
> That day, John took many pictures.

**Panel 2** — Mansoor caught mid-jump in the air, both feet off the ground, grinning wildly.
> Mansoor jumping.

**Panel 3** — Khalil pulling a silly exaggerated face directly at the camera lens.
> Khalil making faces.

**Panel 4** — Ahmed carefully smoothing down his hair with his hands before the photo, very serious about his appearance.
> Ahmed fixing his hair.

**Panel 5** — John quietly angling the camera toward Thuraya, who is unaware, looking off to the side in her white scarf, watching the children with a calm patient expression.
> Even Thuraya, secretly — caught looking somewhere else, her face revealing something true.


### Scene 5 — The Darkroom

**Panel 1** — Evening, inside the house. Windows shut, lamps dimmed, the room cast in deep shadow. John stands very serious, gesturing to the space.
> "This," he said, "is the darkroom."

**Panel 2** — Sophia carefully holding a sheet of photographic paper with both hands under the dim light.
> Sophia held a paper. Carefully.

**Panel 3** — Thuraya leaning in close, watching the process with wide, curious eyes, seeing this for the first time.
> Thuraya watched. With curiosity.

**Panel 4** — The paper is slid into a shallow tray of liquid; all three lean over it, waiting, faces lit faintly from below.
> They put the paper in liquid. Nothing — yet.

**Panel 5** — A faint image slowly emerging on the paper in the tray, ghostly and gray.
> Then slowly… a face appeared.

**Panel 6** — Sophia pointing excitedly at the tray, delighted.
> Sophia gasped: "It's Mansoor!"

**Panel 7** — Another photo develops showing the goat's face; Sophia laughs, pointing again.
> "It's Malood!"

**Panel 8** — A third photo develops, revealing Thuraya's own face; she laughs in surprise at seeing herself.
> "It's you!" Thuraya laughed.

**Panel 9** — Rows of developed photographs strung up to dry along a line in the small dim room, the wall now covered in images.
> The wall was full of memories.

**Panel 10** — John sitting down, tired but content, looking at the wall of photos; Sophia beside him smiling softly.
> John: "These are our days." Sophia: "And we almost lost them to a goat."

**Panel 11** — Through the open window, the silhouette of Malood bleating outside in the dark yard; John closes his eyes, half-smiling.
> "Me-e-e-e!" — John: "That goat will be in every picture."


### Scene 6 — A Wall of Days

**Panel 1** — Kitchen wall strung with a line between two nails, Sophia and Thuraya pinning up photographs one by one with wooden clothes-pegs.
> They hung the photos one by one along the string.

**Panel 2** — First photo pinned up: Mansoor mid-jump, both feet off the ground, face full of unrestrained joy.
> The first photograph: Mansoor mid-jump.

**Panel 3** — Second photo: Malood with the camera in his mouth, looking toward John with an almost satisfied expression.
> The second: Malood, camera in mouth, looking at John.

**Panel 4** — Third photo: Sadou holding Malood like a football, triumphant grin on his face.
> The third: Sadou holding Malood like a football.

**Panel 5** — Fourth and fifth photos side by side: Ahmed sitting in stiff, careful ruler-straight posture; Khalil pulling a face.
> The fourth: Ahmed's careful posture. The fifth: Khalil making a face.

**Panel 6** — Sixth photo: Noura mid-motion, finger pointed at something just outside the frame, captured a beat too fast for the camera.
> The sixth: Noura pointing, her finger faster than the camera.

**Panel 7** — Seventh photo, set slightly apart: Saeed alone in a corner of a group shot, quiet, watching from the edge.
> The seventh: Saeed. In the corner. Watching.

**Panel 8** — Thuraya standing still before the seventh photograph, studying it for a long moment, her expression thoughtful.
> Thuraya stopped at the seventh photograph. "Saeed," she said, quietly.

**Panel 9** — Sophia standing beside her, nodding, both women looking at the same photo.
> Sophia: "He is in many of them. Always at the side. Always watching."

**Panel 10** — Thuraya's face softening into a small private smile as she speaks.
> "He is collecting again. But this time… in the picture, not from outside it."

**Panel 11** — John walks over drying his hands on a cloth, stops beside them, studies the photo of Saeed thoughtfully.
> John: "In the next pictures, he will be in the middle. Not the side."

**Panel 12** — Wide final shot: the whole kitchen wall glowing softly in yellow lamplight, rows of photographs pinned up, John, Sophia, and Thuraya standing before it together; outside, Malood bleats once in the dark yard.
> Sophia: "You think?" John: "I will make sure." — the first photographs of a place that would become his.


---


## Palm Twenty-Five — The Day Nobody Spoke Arabic

### Scene 1 — The Tired Fan and the Important Envelope

**Panel 1** — Wide establishing shot of the classroom: an old standing fan wobbles and rattles in the corner, twelve Omani children seated at their desks.
> "The fan sounded like it had a fever."

**Panel 2** — John stands near his desk, holding a big brown manila envelope carefully, almost reverently, like a treasure chest.
> John holds the envelope like a treasure chest.

**Panel 3** — Sophia stands beside John, biting back a smile, clearly in on a secret.
> Sophia stood next to him, trying very hard not to smile.

**Panel 4** — Thuraya leans against the classroom wall, arms crossed, scanning the children's faces.
> Thuraya leaned against the wall, watching the students' faces.

**Panel 5** — Malood the goat sits near the window like a long-term, never-graduating student.
> Malood sat near the window, like a student who never left school.

**Panel 6** — All twelve children sit bolt upright at their desks, tense and curious, eyes on the envelope.
> The children sat very, very straight.

**Panel 7** — John clears his throat, addressing the class solemnly.
> "Today," he said, slowly, "is a very special day."

**Panel 8** — Mansoor half-rises from his seat, eyes on the envelope, whispering eagerly.
> Mansoor whispered, "Pictures?"

**Panel 9** — John raises one finger in warning, envelope still unopened in his other hand.
> "Yes. But… there is a rule."

### Scene 2 — English Only

**Panel 1** — John stands firm, pointing sharply toward the classroom door as he lays down the rule.
> "No Arabic. Only English... outside."

**Panel 2** — Wide shot: the whole classroom frozen still, children's faces stunned.
> The classroom froze.

**Panel 3** — Close-up on Ali, throat tight, swallowing hard.
> Ali swallowed.

**Panel 4** — Close-up on Noura clapping a hand over her own mouth.
> Noura put her hand over her mouth.

**Panel 5** — Khalil clenches his jaw like he's bracing for battle; beside him Ahmed sits ruler-straight, ready and focused.
> Khalil looked like he was preparing for war.

**Panel 6** — Matla smiles confidently; Rahma and Salma exchange a nervous glance; Marwa whispers under her breath.
> Marwa whispered, "Oh no."

**Panel 7** — John writes on the chalkboard in big letters: "ENGLISH ONLY TODAY," then adds sentence starters below.
> ENGLISH ONLY TODAY — This is… / He is… / She is…

**Panel 8** — Sophia claps her hands, smiling at the silent room; even Malood says nothing.
> "Okay! Who wants to start?" Silence.

**Panel 9** — John pulls the first photo from the envelope and holds it up — it's Malood, appearing in shot after shot (yard, table, door, eating, sleeping); the class bursts into laughter.
> Malood was everywhere. The class exploded with laughter.

**Panel 10** — Mansoor leaps to his feet, arm shooting up.
> Mansoor jumped up. "Me! Me!"

**Panel 11** — Mansoor takes a deep breath and points at the photo, forming his sentence carefully.
> "This is… Malood." John clapped. "Good! Very good!"

**Panel 12** — Mansoor points again, finishing the sentence; Sophia nods approvingly; Malood puffs up with pride.
> "He is… eating!" "Yes! He is eating." Malood looked proud.

### Scene 3 — Smoke, Maghbar, and the First Slip

**Panel 1** — Photo reveal: Sheikh Khalaf stands by his clay house, throwing smoke into the air, performing maghbar; several children gasp.
> Sheikh Khalaf. Doing maghbar. Some students gasped.

**Panel 2** — Close-up on Ali leaning to whisper to a neighbor, mouth mid-word in Arabic.
> Ali whispered, in Arabic: «هوه…»

**Panel 3** — John points sharply at the door; Ali's face freezes in horror, realizing his mistake.
> John pointed. "Outside." Ali froze.

**Panel 4** — Ali, panicked, waves his hands and stammers, then straightens up and tries again slowly.
> "No no no! Sorry! Sorry!" ... "This is… Sheikh Khalaf."

**Panel 5** — Khalil adds on, gesturing at the smoke in the photo; John nods approvingly.
> "He is… making smoke." John nodded. "Yes."

**Panel 6** — Sophia gestures at the sentence structures on the board while Thuraya translates for the kids, then repeats in English for John.
> Thuraya: "They used to do this to chase bad luck."

**Panel 7** — Next photo reveal: John himself, eyes red and swollen, dust caked on his face, looking like an exhausted ghost; the class roars with laughter.
> His eyes were red and swollen. The class laughed again.

**Panel 8** — Noura stands, raises her hand properly first, then points at the photo of John's eyes.
> "This is… Mr. John." "He is… not happy."

**Panel 9** — John sighs; Ahmed politely raises a correction with visible pride in his grammar.
> John: "He was not happy." Ahmed: "He was not happy."

### Scene 4 — Yellow Ladies and the Door

**Panel 1** — Photo reveal: Safiya and Fatkat, both faces stained bright yellow from a saffron mask — Fatkat's mouth hangs open in shock, Safiya forces a smile; the class collapses laughing.
> Both of them. Yellow. Like lemons.

**Panel 2** — Marwa stands to describe the photo, thinking carefully before finishing her sentence.
> "This is… Miss Sophia and Miss Fatkat." "They are… yellow."

**Panel 3** — Fatkat, standing near the classroom door, throws up her hands and shouts in Arabic, scandalized.
> Fatkat shouted: «يا شيخة فضيحة!» (Oh dear, what a scandal!)

**Panel 4** — Safiya laughs it off with a shrug; Thuraya chimes in with a pun.
> Safiya: "Skin care." Thuraya: "But the skin doesn't care."

**Panel 5** — Mohammadeen pauses in the doorway mid-stride, laughing, calling out in Egyptian Arabic.
> Mohammadeen: «والله عاملين زي الليمون.» (I swear they look just like lemons.)

**Panel 6** — Montage panel: photo of Ali proudly holding up a fish; beside it, Khalil pulling a silly face for the camera.
> Ali holding a fish. Khalil making a silly face.

**Panel 7** — Montage panel: Matla posing like a queen; Rahma and Salma standing close together, shy.
> Matla standing like a queen. Rahma and Salma, shy.

**Panel 8** — Montage panel: Marwa mid-fall near the falaj water channel; Noura pointing at something offscreen, shouting.
> Marwa falling near the falaj. Noura pointing and shouting.

**Panel 9** — Montage panel: Saeed quietly visible in the corner of several photos, always watching; Ahmed shown helping a classmate; Mansoor caught mid-run in nearly every frame.
> Saeed watching. Ahmed helping. Mansoor running.

### Scene 5 — A Whole Class in English

**Panel 1** — Ali stands beside his photo, gesturing at the fish in the picture.
> "This is Ali." "He is holding a fish."

**Panel 2** — Matla stands tall and poised next to her photo.
> "This is Matla." "She is standing."

**Panel 3** — Khalil scratches his head, searching for the word, Ahmed and Thuraya leaning in to help him find it.
> "This is Khalil." "He is… uh… funny."

**Panel 4** — Malood, sitting among the children, opens his mouth and bleats loudly — the only sound in the room not in English.
> Malood only made a "meeeeh" sound.

**Panel 5** — Photo reveal: Sadoo clutching Malood tightly to his chest like a hero rescuing him; Safiya narrates, John nods approvingly.
> "This is Sadoo. He is saving the camera." "Yes. Very brave."

**Panel 6** — Series of candid photos revealed: the class caught unaware in the shop, in the street, playing, running.
> Photos taken secretly — in the shop, in the street, playing, running.

**Panel 7** — Wide shot of the children staring at the photos, realizing they'd been secretly photographed all along.
> The students stared.

**Panel 8** — Matla, puzzled, asks slowly, pointing at the candid photos; Thuraya answers with a knowing smile.
> Matla: "Teacher… when?" Thuraya: "He took them secretly."

**Panel 9** — All eyes turn to John, who puts on an exaggerated innocent expression, chest puffed with pride.
> "I am a photographer," he said. Proudly.

### Scene 6 — The Bell, the Tunisian, and Saeed's Smile

**Panel 1** — The school bell rings loudly; every child in the room jumps in their seat.
> Drrrrrrrrrriiiiiiing! Everyone jumped.

**Panel 2** — Mansoor shouts out instinctively in Arabic, breaking the day's rule without thinking.
> Mansoor shouted: «طلاااقة!» (A shot!)

**Panel 3** — In the corridor outside, Rabaa the Tunisian music teacher clutches her chest in alarm, mid-scream.
> Rabaa screamed: «يا ربّ! من ضرب؟!» (Who shot?!)

**Panel 4** — Thuraya laughs and reassures Rabaa, who is still catching her breath by the doorway.
> Thuraya: "Not gun. School finish." Rabaa: "You scared me!"

**Panel 5** — Wide shot of the children streaming out of the classroom into the yard, shouting, laughing, singing together.
> The students ran out, shouting, laughing, singing.

**Panel 6** — Inside, John slumps into his chair, tired but content; Safiya sits beside him.
> Safiya: "Today… they spoke English." Thuraya: "And nobody died."

**Panel 7** — Malood leaps onto the desk and grabs a photo in his mouth; John jumps up shouting as Malood bolts, everyone laughing.
> John shouted, "No!" Malood ran. Everyone laughed.

**Panel 8** — Outside, Saeed pauses alone in the doorway, looking back at the classroom, the scattered photos on the desk, and Thuraya.
> Saeed stood for a moment, looking back.

**Panel 9** — Saeed's face softens into a small private smile before he turns and runs off after the other children.
> Then he smiled, and ran after the others.


---


## Palm Twenty-Six — The Detective Game and the Missing Treasure

### Scene 1 — When Thuraya Smiles Like That

**Panel 1** — A modest village classroom, unusually still. Students at their desks, wide-eyed, watching the teacher instead of fidgeting.
> "It was quiet because Ms. Thuraya was smiling."

**Panel 2** — Close-up on Thuraya's face: a small, conspiratorial third kind of smile, distinct from her warm or teacherly smiles.
> The dangerous smile — a game is coming.

**Panel 3** — Wide shot of the room: John by the blackboard with chalk, Safiya at the small teacher's desk fixing a notebook, Mohammadeen leaning arms-crossed against the wall, Fatkat fanning herself with a book, Malood the baby goat chewing something odd by the window.
> Everyone in position, unaware of what's coming.

**Panel 4** — Thuraya claps her hands once, addressing the class in English.
> "Today, we will not sit and write."

**Panel 5** — Mansoor half-falling out of his chair, hopeful.
> "Teacher… we go home?"

**Panel 6** — John, laughing, shaking his head at Mansoor.
> "No, Mansoor. Worse."

**Panel 7** — The class groaning collectively, then bursting into excited chatter as Thuraya announces the game — Noura, Marwa, and Mansoor all talking over each other.
> "A game?" "A real game?" "A running game?"

**Panel 8** — Thuraya raising a hand for quiet, delivering the big reveal.
> "A detective game."

**Panel 9** — Thuraya at her bag, carefully unfolding a large, old-looking hand-drawn paper map in front of the gathering class.
> A map — "the map of today's mystery."

**Panel 10** — Safiya leaning forward eagerly; John studying the map with mock seriousness.
> "Is there… danger?" — "Always."

---

### Scene 2 — The Treasure That Is Hidden

**Panel 1** — Students crowding around the map, even stiff, disciplined Ahmed leaning in with rare curiosity.
> Even Ahmed leans in.

**Panel 2** — Thuraya pointing to a drawing of a tall palm tree on the map.
> "Somewhere, there is a treasure."

**Panel 3** — Khalil whispering dramatically; Mansoor jumping up.
> "Treasure?" … "Who stole it?"

**Panel 4** — Thuraya turning her gaze pointedly to Malood the goat, who just blinks — class erupts in laughter.
> A silent joke, instantly understood.

**Panel 5** — John at the blackboard, chalk in hand, writing a list of instruction-phrases: Go straight / Turn left / Turn right / Stop / Don't run / Look under… / Look behind…
> Today's lesson: instructions.

**Panel 6** — Safiya adding a stern rule beside the list; students exchanging fearful glances.
> "And remember: Only English."

**Panel 7** — Mohammadeen slowly raising his stick, declaring solidarity with the rule; classroom falls into stunned silence.
> "Today… I also speak only English."

**Panel 8** — Fatkat smiling sweetly at the visibly nervous kids.
> "Good luck, my children."

**Panel 9** — Thuraya splitting the class into two groups — boys cheering on one side, girls cheering louder on the other.
> Team One (boys) vs. Team Two (girls).

**Panel 10** — John saluting and Mohammadeen nodding seriously as Team One leaders; Safiya smiling proudly and Fatkat cracking her knuckles as Team Two leaders.
> Team leaders named — game faces on.

**Panel 11** — Thuraya patting Malood, who makes a small defiant sound.
> "Malood is with me. For safety."

---

### Scene 3 — The Old Wall and the Falaj

**Panel 1** — Wide establishing shot: sunny, still village lanes, tall quiet palm trees lining the path, both teams heading outside with their instruction papers.
> The village watches, unsurprised as ever.

**Panel 2** — Thuraya handing each team their first paper, with a stern warning look.
> "Don't cheat. Don't fight. And don't die."

**Panel 3** — Team One boys breaking into a run, Mohammadeen shouting at them to stop, boys awkwardly power-walking instead.
> "No running!" — fast-walking begins.

**Panel 4** — Ahmed reading the paper aloud to the boys near an old stone wall; Mansoor triumphantly holding up a found paper.
> "Go straight. Stop at the old wall. Look under the stone."

**Panel 5** — Khalil reading the next clue near a tree, team moving on.
> "Turn right. Walk ten steps. Look behind the tree."

**Panel 6** — Team Two: Safiya reading a clue near the falaj (irrigation channel), Fatkat laughing beside her.
> "Go left. Stop near the falaj. Don't touch the water."

**Panel 7** — Marwa slowly sounding out a clue near a rock, Noura pointing ahead toward a house in the distance.
> "Go straight until you see a house."

**Panel 8** — The girls' team stopping short in front of a particular house — the mood visibly shifts, faces turning uneasy.
> It was the house of Shayb Khalaf.

**Panel 9** — Salima whispering nervously, Fatkat swallowing hard, looking toward the closed door; Thuraya watching from a distance with a carefully innocent expression.
> "Why here?" … "Who made this map?"

**Panel 10** — The girls grabbing a paper from under a tree near the house and sprinting away as fast as they can.
> They ran. Very fast. Nobody wanted to stay.

---

### Scene 4 — The Pickup and the Bees

**Panel 1** — Uncle Nasser seated by his old pickup truck, calmly sipping tea from a bent tin cup, as Team One boys approach.
> Uncle Nasser, same tea, same pickup.

**Panel 2** — Uncle Nasser looking up at the boys; John smiling.
> "What are you doing?" — "Learning English."

**Panel 3** — Uncle Nasser nodding, unbothered, accepting the strangeness without question.
> A nod that explains nothing and everything.

**Panel 4** — Mohammadeen haltingly addressing Uncle Nasser in halting English, checking the clue paper.
> "Please… sir… where is… the fence?"

**Panel 5** — Uncle Nasser laughing, pointing directly behind Mohammadeen.
> "Behind you, ya ustadh."

**Panel 6** — Meanwhile, Team Two girls approaching a big tree, freezing at a buzzing sound.
> Bzzzzzz. Fatkat: "No. No. No. I know this sound."

**Panel 7** — Safiya reading the clue pointing them toward the tree; girls exchanging wary looks while Mansoor's voice calls out from a distance.
> "Look near the tree." … "Maybe it is empty!"

**Panel 8** — Chaos: Mansoor screaming and running, everyone scattering away from the tree in a panic; bees undisturbed above.
> "BEEEEES!" — everyone runs, the bees don't move.

**Panel 9** — Thuraya, watching from afar, calmly jotting a note in her notebook with a small smile.
> Thuraya writes: "Good drama."

---

### Scene 5 — Palm Al-Fard

**Panel 1** — Wide shot: both teams converging from different directions toward one enormous, solitary palm tree towering over the farmland — Palm Al-Fard.
> The lone palm, tallest for miles — the village landmark.

**Panel 2** — Team Two arriving first; Noura pointing straight up, spotting something.
> "There!" — a small bag, very high up.

**Panel 3** — Fatkat clapping triumphantly while Safiya looks up, worried.
> "We win!" — "How do we get it?"

**Panel 4** — Team One arriving moments later; Khalil craning his neck upward, John scratching his head.
> "It's too high." — "We need a hero."

**Panel 5** — Thuraya walking up slowly to both teams gathered at the base of the tree.
> "So, you found it. But… the game is not finished."

**Panel 6** — Everyone tilting their heads back, staring up at the impossibly high bag swaying against the sky.
> Very high. Too high.

**Panel 7** — The bag visibly shifting/moving; Mansoor's face turning worried.
> "Maybe it is a snake."

**Panel 8** — John stepping back cautiously; Thuraya laughing and waving off the fear.
> "Then the game is finished." — "No, no. It is food."

**Panel 9** — Thuraya revealing the contents; students' faces lighting up, Mohammadeen rubbing his hands together.
> "Honey. And ghee." — "Healthy food."

---

### Scene 6 — Who Will Climb?

**Panel 1** — John staring up at the towering palm, stumped, the whole group looking around for a solution.
> "We need… someone who can climb."

**Panel 2** — Everyone turning in unison to look down the road, footsteps approaching.
> All eyes turn toward the road.

**Panel 3** — Sadou strolling into view, carrying a bag, wearing his signature knowing grin.
> "Who needs help?"

**Panel 4** — Group reaction shot: knowing looks exchanged between the villagers at Sadou's suspiciously perfect timing.
> Sadou — always passing by, always knowing everything.

**Panel 5** — Thuraya closing her notebook, addressing both teams with a teasing smile, glancing toward Sadou.
> "Tomorrow, we will continue… how to ask, promise — and convince."

**Panel 6** — Students shouting in dismayed anticipation; Sadou's grin widening.
> "Tomorrow?!"

**Panel 7** — Malood the goat comically charging at the palm trunk and attempting to climb it, failing and sliding back down; the whole group laughing.
> Malood tries to climb. He fails. Everyone laughs.

**Panel 8** — John looking from the tree, to Sadou, to the class, with mock seriousness.
> "Tomorrow will be dangerous."

**Panel 9** — In the background, a thin, quiet figure watches from the shade of a distant tree — the old man from the earlier house, observing unseen.
> Someone old. Someone quiet. Watching.

**Panel 10** — Final wide shot: Shayb Khalaf's door closing slowly in the distance as the sun settles over the village and the treasure-hunters disperse.
> The door closes slowly — an opinion kept for another day.


---


## Palm Twenty-Seven — Convincing Sadou

### Scene 1 — I Will

**Panel 1** — Village school yard at early dawn light; children (Ahmed, Mansoor, Malood, others) arriving far too early, clustering and craning their necks upward.
> "Everyone arrived at school early. Too early."

**Panel 2** — Close-up shot from the yard looking up: a small knotted bag of honey and ghee hangs motionless near the very top of Palm Al-Fard, fifteen metres up, untouched.
> "The treasure. Still hanging. Untouched."

**Panel 3** — Night-to-dawn vignette: Mansoor in sleepwear, peering out a window/door at 3am toward the dark silhouette of the palm tree, disappointed the bag hasn't fallen.
> Mansoor checks at 3 a.m. — the bag is still there.

**Panel 4** — Inside the mud-brick classroom: John stands at a chalkboard writing large block letters, class seated and watching.
> "I WILL"

**Panel 5** — John turns to face the class, gesturing.
> "Today, we learn how to promise."

**Panel 6** — Safiya, Mohammadeen, and Fatkat each stand briefly in turn beside John, adding to the lesson.
> "And how to ask nicely." / "And how to not fight." / "And how to not cry."

**Panel 7** — Mansoor's face, worried, brow furrowed, as if personally called out by all four rules at once.
> Mansoor looks worried — he's guilty of all four.

**Panel 8** — Thuraya at the front of the room claps her hands, smiling, announcing to the class.
> "Today's hero is… Sadou."

**Panel 9** — Cut outside: Sadou sits cross-legged in the shade near a wall, whittling something small with a pocketknife, chin lifted, visibly proud of himself.
> Sadou, sitting in the shade, looking very proud of himself.

**Panel 10** — Malood stands nearby, staring hard at Sadou with narrowed, suspicious goat-eyes, as if sizing up a rival.
> Malood stares at Sadou like he's planning a crime.

### Scene 2 — The Refusal

**Panel 1** — Thuraya leads the whole class out of the classroom into the yard toward Sadou and the palm tree.
> Thuraya takes the class outside.

**Panel 2** — Sadou looks up at the approaching group, arms loosely crossed, amused.
> "Ah. The English army."

**Panel 3** — John greets him; Sadou nods back teasingly; children behind them burst out laughing.
> "Morning… mister teacher… mister long."

**Panel 4** — John points upward at the palm tree, Sadou following his gaze.
> "We need your help."

**Panel 5** — Sadou looks up at the bag, recognition dawning.
> "Aha. The bag."

**Panel 6** — Safiya steps forward, explaining; Sadou's eyes brighten with shopkeeper interest.
> "Inside the bag is honey and ghee."

**Panel 7** — Fatkat leans toward another student, whispering, watching Sadou's face closely.
> "He is interested."

**Panel 8** — Thuraya explains they can't climb; Sadou crosses his arms firmly, unmoved.
> "But Sadou, we cannot climb." / "And?"

**Panel 9** — John asks him directly; Sadou glances at the class, then the tree, then at Malood, and flatly refuses.
> "We want you to climb and get it." / "No."

**Panel 10** — The whole group freezes in shock; Mansoor throws his hands up, shouting.
> "No?! Why no?!"

**Panel 11** — Sadou smiles slyly, enjoying his advantage; Khalil whispers to a neighbor.
> "Because." — "He is enjoying this."

**Panel 12** — Thuraya's slow, knowing smile spreads as she turns back to her students, raising her hand to start the lesson.
> "Okay. Let's begin the lesson." — "I will!"

### Scene 3 — The Great Persuasion

**Panel 1** — Noura steps up to Sadou carefully, offering a plate gesture; Sadou doesn't even look at her.
> "Sadou… I will give you samboosa." / "No."

**Panel 2** — Ali tries next, hopeful; Sadou shakes his head.
> "I will give you bakoorah." / "No."

**Panel 3** — Marwa and Rahma each step up in turn offering dates, then bread; Sadou refuses both flatly.
> "I will give you dates." / "I will give you bread." / "No."

**Panel 4** — Salima offers tea; Sadou smiles this time, shaking his head.
> "I don't like tea."

**Panel 5** — Khalil offers fish; Sadou bursts out laughing.
> "I will give you fish." / "No."

**Panel 6** — Ahmed politely offers to help in the shop; Sadou asks a sharp follow-up; Ahmed freezes, caught off guard.
> "For how long?" / "One… day?" / "No."

**Panel 7** — Mansoor leaps forward dramatically, pointing at Malood; chaos erupts, Malood screaming loudest, Thuraya shouting in alarm.
> "I will give you Malood!"

**Panel 8** — Sadou doubled over laughing, nearly toppling backward; Malood standing off to the side looking deeply offended.
> "I don't want this trouble."

**Panel 9** — Safiya sighs in defeat; Mohammadeen and Fatkat chime in with mock-serious suggestions.
> "Nothing works." / "In Egypt, we solve this with shouting." / "And sometimes shoes."

### Scene 4 — Saeed's Whisper

**Panel 1** — John stands still, deep in thought, eyes moving from the class, to Sadou, to the tree, to Saeed.
> John was thinking. Very hard.

**Panel 2** — Saeed stands apart from the group, quiet and watchful as always, observing everything.
> Saeed, standing quietly, watching everything.

**Panel 3** — John crosses to Saeed and leans down, whispering something in his ear.
> John whispers something in his ear.

**Panel 4** — Saeed's eyes go wide, then a slow smile spreads across his face.
> Saeed's eyes became wide. He smiled. Slowly.

**Panel 5** — Saeed walks calmly toward Sadou; the whole yard falls silent, watching.
> Everyone became quiet.

**Panel 6** — Saeed looks up at Sadou and speaks carefully in simple English.
> "Sadou… I will give you… chilli. Very hot."

**Panel 7** — Thuraya quickly steps in to correct his phrasing; the class bursts out laughing.
> "Chilli, not filfil yohroak."

**Panel 8** — Sadou's eyes open wide with sudden interest; Saeed nods seriously back at him.
> "Chilli?" / "Yes. Big chilli. Very strong."

**Panel 9** — A slow, dangerous grin spreads across Sadou's face — the look of a true chilli-lover.
> Sadou's face changed. A slow, dangerous smile appeared.

**Panel 10** — Sadou stands up abruptly, hands on hips, half-scolding.
> "You have chilli?" / "Why you not say this from beginning?"

**Panel 11** — The whole class erupts, boys and girls jumping and shouting, Mansoor dancing with joy.
> "He agreed! We win!"

**Panel 12** — Fatkat raises a hand to halt the celebration; girls cross their arms on one side, boys on the other, as Thuraya announces the boys' win.
> "The boys convinced Sadou." — girls groan, Mansoor dances.

### Scene 5 — The Great Climb

**Panel 1** — Sadou ties his waist-cloth tighter, sizes up the palm tree, and cracks his knuckles in preparation.
> Sadou tied his cloth tighter, cracked his fingers.

**Panel 2** — Sadou begins climbing the palm trunk with practiced, cat-like ease, feet flat against the bark, hips pushed outward.
> Then he started climbing. Like a cat.

**Panel 3** — Wide shot from below: the whole class watches with mouths open as Sadou ascends rapidly.
> Up. Up. Up. Students watch with open mouths.

**Panel 4** — Safiya and John exchange amazed whispers, still staring upward.
> "He is not human." / "He is… many talents."

**Panel 5** — Sadou reaches the very top of the palm in under a minute and grabs the bag.
> In one minute, Sadou reached the top. He took the bag.

**Panel 6** — Sadou looks down at the crowd below, calling out; the class shouts back eagerly.
> "Ready?" / "YES!"

**Panel 7** — Sadou hurls the bag downward; Mohammadeen lunges and misses, Fatkat lunges and misses.
> He threw the bag. Mohammadeen missed. Fatkat missed.

**Panel 8** — Malood leaps to catch it, gets struck by the falling bag, and topples over.
> The bag hit Malood. Malood fell.

**Panel 9** — The bag bursts open on the ground; the honey jar and ghee jar go rolling across the dirt as everyone screams.
> The bag opened. The jars rolled. Everyone screamed.

**Panel 10** — John, Thuraya, and Mansoor all sprint toward the rolling jars in a panic.
> John ran. Thuraya ran. Mansoor ran.

**Panel 11** — Relief: the group sits collapsed on the ground around the intact jars, chests heaving as if they'd run a marathon.
> Somehow, nothing broke. They sat, breathing hard.

**Panel 12** — Safiya looks at the unbroken jars beside her, exhaling.
> "We are alive."

### Scene 6 — Healthy Food — and a Small Watching Shadow

**Panel 1** — The class files back into the classroom; Thuraya sets the honey and ghee jars down on the front table.
> They went back to school. Thuraya put the jars on the table.

**Panel 2** — Thuraya announces the lesson while John writes on the chalkboard behind her.
> "Today's lesson is Healthy Food." — "Honey is healthy. Ghee is healthy. Too much sugar is not healthy."

**Panel 3** — Mansoor raises his hand with a mischievous grin; Fatkat laughs beside him.
> "Honey and ghee… together?" / "That is not a lesson. That is a crime."

**Panel 4** — Students gather around the table making small pieces of bread, dipping and tasting.
> They made small bread and tasted a little.

**Panel 5** — John closes his eyes, savoring a bite, rubbing his stomach ruefully.
> "Now I understand… why Omani men have big belly."

**Panel 6** — Thuraya laughs, gesturing proudly; Safiya looks puzzled beside her.
> "It's good for wearing khanjar!" / "What is khanjar?"

**Panel 7** — The whole classroom dissolves into overlapping chatter and laughter, lesson forgotten, everyone talking at once.
> Everyone started talking at the same time.

**Panel 8** — Sadou appears in the doorway; Saeed steps up and hands him a small chilli pepper, smiling.
> Sadou stood at the door. Saeed gave him a small chilli.

**Panel 9** — Sadou bites into the chilli confidently, then his face contorts and he bolts away in pain.
> Then he bit it. Then he screamed. Then he ran.

**Panel 10** — The whole class doubles over laughing at the sight of their shopkeeper-neighbor fleeing from a tiny chilli.
> Everyone laughed — the story had ended exactly as it should.

**Panel 11** — Later, the empty classroom in soft afternoon light; Thuraya sits alone at her desk, writing quietly in a notebook.
> "Today, they learned how to promise. And how to convince. And how to work together."

**Panel 12** — Wide establishing shot of the village at dusk: near an old clay house in the distance, a still, silent figure watches from the shade — revealed as Shayb Khalaf.
> Far away, near the old house, a shadow watched. Shayb Khalaf.


---


## Palm Twenty-Eight — We Must and We Mustn't — The Confession Lesson

### Scene One — The Punching Sun

**Panel 1** — Wide establishing shot of the Samail school yard under a blazing sun; heat shimmer, tired palm leaves, empty yard, oppressive stillness.
> "The sun in Samail did not shine. It punched."

**Panel 2** — Mr. John walking slowly across the yard, holding a small chalkboard, his face serious and deliberate, moving at a conserved, unhurried pace.
> Mr. John crosses the yard, chalkboard in hand, moving at "the speed of conservation."

**Panel 3** — Safiya walking behind John, carrying a notebook and water bottle, dressed practically for the heat, calm resigned expression.
> Safiya follows with notebook and water bottle, dressed for the heat at last.

**Panel 4** — Ms. Thuraya already seated cross-legged on a woven mat under the palm tree, chalk in hand, eyes bright and ready.
> Thuraya waits under the palm tree, chalk ready, "teacher-heart very, very ready."

**Panel 5** — Malood the baby goat darting between the adults' feet, stopping to stare at and nibble the corner of John's chalkboard.
> "Malood," John said slowly, "this is not salad."

**Panel 6** — Thuraya clapping once, calling the children to sit in a circle under the palm tree.
> "Okay, class! Sit down. Circle. Under the palm tree."

**Panel 7** — The students — Ali, Ahmed, Khalil, Noura, Marwa, Rahma, Salimah, Matla, Mansoor, and smaller kids — running in a messy line toward the mat.
> The students come running in a messy line, like curious birds.

**Panel 8** — The children now seated in a circle, legs crossed, backs half-straight, eyes shining, some holding notebooks.
> Legs crossed, eyes shining — some hold notebooks, some hold only confidence.

**Panel 9** — Close-up of John writing "WE MUST / WE MUSTN'T" in big chalk letters on the wooden-stand chalkboard, wiping sweat from his brow.
> John chalks: WE MUST / WE MUSTN'T.

**Panel 10** — Safiya and Thuraya exchanging a knowing look, Safiya nodding proudly, Thuraya smiling mischievously.
> Thuraya smiled. "Also… it will be funny."

---

### Scene Two — John Confesses First

**Panel 1** — John standing before the circle of seated children, announcing something bold, kids leaning forward in anticipation.
> "Today… we do something brave."

**Panel 2** — John pointing to his own chest, children's mouths open in a collective gasp.
> "I will start. I confess."

**Panel 3** — John miming carrying a bucket and spilling water on his own shoe, sheepish expression.
> "Yesterday… I tried to carry a bucket of water… and I spilled it on my shoe."

**Panel 4** — Mansoor whispering loudly to a neighbor, unimpressed look on his face, other kids stifling laughter.
> Mansoor whispered loudly. "That's it?"

**Panel 5** — Whole class bursting into laughter, John sighing good-naturedly beside the chalkboard.
> Everyone laughed; John sighed, "Okay. Not a big crime."

**Panel 6** — Thuraya speaking softly to the group, gentle expression, framed against the palm tree.
> Thuraya added, softly, "Sometimes we learn English with our mistakes."

**Panel 7** — Safiya clapping once, explaining the sentence pattern with an enthusiastic gesture toward the chalkboard.
> "Today you say: I did… and I feel… Then we say: We must… or We mustn't…"

**Panel 8** — John pointing to the board, mouth open mid-word, class echoing back in unison, some with hands cupped like a chant.
> Class echoed: "We must tell the truth."

**Panel 9** — A dramatic pause — all children silent and still, eyes wide, waiting.
> A dramatic silence.

**Panel 10** — Khalil raising his hand with an exaggeratedly innocent face, standing up with hands behind his back like a tiny defendant.
> Khalil stood. "I… I did… a bad thing."

---

### Scene Three — The Bucket Crime

**Panel 1** — Khalil standing tall in the circle, repeating his line proudly, chest puffed out.
> Khalil repeated, proud. "I did a bad thing."

**Panel 2** — Khalil pointing one finger dramatically at the classroom door, like presenting courtroom evidence.
> Khalil points to the classroom door — "Yesterday… Teacher Thuraya… she came… to open the door."

**Panel 3** — Thuraya's knowing look and Safiya leaning forward with keen interest, both already suspecting the story.
> Safiya leaned in. "Oh no."

**Panel 4** — Khalil miming placing a bucket above a door frame with his hands.
> "And I put… a bucket… above the door."

**Panel 5** — Mansoor exploding into laughter, doubled over, unable to hold it in; Khalil grinning and raising his chin, arms out mimicking a splash — "BOOSH."
> Khalil: "And water… went… BOOSH… on her head."

**Panel 6** — The whole circle of children laughing, even the youngest ones giggling without fully understanding why; Thuraya trying to hide a smile, corner of her mouth twitching, arms crossed.
> Thuraya tried to look angry, but her mouth betrayed her.

**Panel 7** — John holding up a hand for quiet, then writing two new sentences on the chalkboard.
> John chalks: We mustn't play dangerous jokes. We must respect our teachers.

**Panel 8** — Khalil repeating carefully, index finger raised as if making a solemn vow.
> Khalil, careful: "We mustn't play dangerous jokes."

**Panel 9** — Full class chanting the second rule together, rhythmic and chorus-like.
> The students echoed it like a chant: "We must respect our teachers."

**Panel 10** — Thuraya with arms folded looking at Khalil, Khalil nodding fast and earnest; Malood bleating loudly nearby as if joining in, Safiya laughing.
> Safiya laughed. "Even Malood agrees."

---

### Scene Four — Bob Jamid

**Panel 1** — Mansoor's hand shooting up first, standing so fast he nearly trips over Rahma's notebook on the ground.
> Mansoor stood up so fast he almost tripped over Rahma's notebook.

**Panel 2** — Mansoor standing with wide dramatic eyes, visibly nervous but determined to speak.
> "I… I confess," he said, in broken English. But brave.

**Panel 3** — Mansoor recounting his trip to Sadoo's shop, gesturing as if walking there; Thuraya's eyes flicking toward him warily.
> Mansoor: "I… go… to Sadoo shop." Thuraya: "Uh-oh."

**Panel 4** — Mansoor miming grabbing a fizzy drink bottle quickly, words rushing out; Safiya looking puzzled beside Thuraya who whispers an explanation.
> "I take… Bob Jamid." Thuraya whispers: "A cold drink. Fizzy."

**Panel 5** — Mansoor nodding hard, admitting he didn't pay; the circle of children reacting with a collective shocked "OHHHH," Ahmed sitting bolt upright, Ali raising his eyebrows.
> "I take… without pay." The class: "OHHHH."

**Panel 6** — John staying calm and composed, writing new rules on the chalkboard while the class watches.
> John chalks: We must pay for things. We mustn't steal.

**Panel 7** — Full class echoing the two rules loudly, some pumping a fist slightly with each line.
> The class echoed, louder: "We mustn't steal."

**Panel 8** — Mansoor taking a deep breath, standing straight like a soldier making a vow, hand near his chest.
> "I mustn't… steal again."

**Panel 9** — Safiya crouching slightly to speak gently to Mansoor, encouraging tone and gesture.
> Safiya: "And you can fix it. You can say sorry."

**Panel 10** — Mansoor nodding firmly, Thuraya pointing at him with a stern-but-warm look; the other kids beginning to clap and smile.
> "And I pay!" — the kids clapped like a happy ending.

---

### Scene Five — More Confessions

**Panel 1** — Noura standing up eagerly among the seated circle, hand still raised, bright and talkative expression.
> Noura stood. "Teacher… I confess… I talk too much."

**Panel 2** — Class laughing warmly, Noura laughing at herself too, hand on her own chest.
> Noura: "I… I don't stop." The class laughed with her.

**Panel 3** — John writing a new pair of rules on the board while Noura places her hand over her heart like taking an oath.
> John chalks: We must listen. We mustn't interrupt.

**Panel 4** — Rahma standing shyly, looking down at her feet, twisting her hands, hesitant but determined.
> Rahma, shy: "I confess… I broke… my sister's comb."

**Panel 5** — Rahma admitting she blamed the cat, other children giggling around her; she looks down, embarrassed.
> "And I said… cat did it." Everyone giggled.

**Panel 6** — Rahma sitting back down after repeating the new rule softly, posture visibly lighter and relieved; Thuraya watching her closely with a knowing, tender expression.
> John chalks: We must take responsibility. We mustn't blame others. Rahma sits, lighter.

**Panel 7** — Montage panel: Marwa gesturing about hiding homework under a mat, mother figure implied off-panel.
> Marwa confessed she hid her homework under a mat.

**Panel 8** — Montage panel: Salimah looking sheepish, hands clasped, recounting teasing her brother about his ears; Ali beside her looking thoughtful, Ahmed nodding seriously.
> Salimah admitted teasing her brother's ears to make him cry.

**Panel 9** — Montage panel: Matla standing proudly, holding out her best pencil to the group like an offering, small "businesswoman" confidence.
> Matla confessed keeping two pencils, then offers her best pencil as "compensation."

**Panel 10** — The smallest children taking turns standing to confess tiny things — one miming eating sugar, another kicking a pretend stone, another throwing a date seed toward Malood, who lifts his head proudly.
> Small children confess: sugar before lunch, a kicked stone, a date seed thrown at the goat.

---

### Scene Six — The Strong Ending

**Panel 1** — John clapping his hands together, beaming at the whole seated circle.
> "Excellent. Today you used English for real life."

**Panel 2** — John gesturing toward the chalkboard covered in the day's rules, class sitting attentively.
> "You spoke truth. You learned rules."

**Panel 3** — Full class reciting all the rules together in unison, chalkboard visible behind them listing each line.
> Class chants all six rules together, like a strong ending.

**Panel 4** — Safiya and Thuraya exchanging a warm smile over the children's heads.
> Safiya smiled at Thuraya. "This lesson is… powerful."

**Panel 5** — Thuraya nodding, amused, watching the circle of kids.
> Thuraya nodded. "And funny."

**Panel 6** — John picking up the chalkboard, telling the class to go wash their hands; kids already scrambling up.
> "Now… everyone go wash your hands."

**Panel 7** — Kids repeating the phrase back automatically while laughing and standing, Malood trying to bite the chalk again.
> Kids laugh: "We must wash our hands!" Malood tries to bite the chalk again.

**Panel 8** — John yanking the chalk away from Malood quickly, mock-serious face.
> "And we mustn't eat chalk!"

**Panel 9** — Wide shot of children roaring with laughter as a distant bell rings; one child shouting and the group leaping up like springs, running toward the classroom.
> "Tolqah!" — the students jump up like springs, running toward shade.

**Panel 10** — Closing shot: Thuraya standing alone under the palm tree watching the children go, Safiya leaning close beside her, both smiling; John in the background, chalkboard under his arm.
> Thuraya: "They are learning English… but also how to be good humans."

**Panel 11** — Safiya and Thuraya looking down at Malood, who stands nearby looking pleased with himself.
> Safiya: "And Malood?" Thuraya: "Malood is learning… how to cause trouble in two languages."

**Panel 12** — Final wide shot: the three adults together under the palm tree, John sighing with a smile, palm leaves swaying gently above in golden light.
> John sighed. "That goat will become fluent before me."


---


## Palm Twenty-Nine — We Should and We Shouldn't — And the Grasshopper Wing

### Scene 1 — A Kinder Sun

**Panel 1** — Wide establishing shot of Samail village in late-afternoon golden light; clay walls glowing honey-colored, long shadows, the big palm tree with woven mats spread beneath it.
> "The afternoon in Samail had a different personality."

**Panel 2** — Mr. John arrives alone, carrying a chalkboard under one arm and a small cup of water, looking up at the palm tree.
> "This tree is becoming our second classroom."

**Panel 3** — Safiya, already seated on a mat, smiling up at John as wind stirs the palm fronds overhead.
> "And a very windy one."

**Panel 4** — Ms. Thuraya walking with calm, steady steps; behind her a loose group of students laughing and talking as they approach.
> The students arrive, laughing.

**Panel 5** — Malood swaggering ahead of the group, dropping onto the mat and chewing on something unidentifiable, unbothered.
> Malood walked like he owned the place.

**Panel 6** — John looking at Malood with tired exasperation, pointing at whatever he's chewing.
> "Malood, you should not eat mystery food."

**Panel 7** — Wide shot: all students seated in a circle on the mats — Ali, Ahmed, Khalil, Noura, Marwa, Rahma, Salimah, Matla, Mansoor, and smaller children clustered at the edges.
> The students sat in a circle.

**Panel 8** — Safiya standing, clapping her hands to get the group's attention, students looking up at her.
> "Okay everyone. Today we talk about good things."

**Panel 9** — John chalking large letters on the board — "WE SHOULD / WE SHOULDN'T" — while Thuraya looks on, smiling.
> Today's grammar would be about pride, not guilt.

### Scene 2 — Things to Be Proud Of

**Panel 1** — John standing before the seated circle, pointing to his own chest as he begins the exercise.
> "Today you will tell us good things you are proud of. I start."

**Panel 2** — John miming a wince from strong coffee; students in the circle laughing openly, some covering their mouths.
> "I am proud that I learned to drink Omani coffee without crying."

**Panel 3** — Safiya, arms crossed teasingly, adding a jab; John nodding back at her seriously.
> "And you should stop putting three spoons of sugar in it." "Yes. I should."

**Panel 4** — Matla's hand shooting straight up before Thuraya even finishes asking; she rises to her feet, standing very straight.
> "I am proud that I give my pencils to Rahma when she forget."

**Panel 5** — Safiya gently correcting Matla's grammar with an encouraging smile; Matla repeating the corrected line carefully.
> "I am proud that I gave my pencils."

**Panel 6** — John writing "We should help others" on the board as the class repeats it aloud; beside Matla, Rahma leans in and whispers.
> Rahma whispered, "Thank you."

**Panel 7** — Ali standing to speak, his father's letters implied in his posture — proud but quiet; Thuraya watching him with a reflective expression.
> "I am proud that I help my father read letters."

**Panel 8** — John writing "We should help our family" on the board; Ali sitting back down, visibly taller/prouder.
> "Excellent."

**Panel 9** — Ahmed standing with hands clasped behind his back, delivering his line; the class reacting with exaggerated disbelief, Mansoor smirking.
> "I am proud that I do my homework before playing." "Ooooooh." "Impossible."

**Panel 10** — John chalking the sentence on the board while Mansoor stares at the ground, sulking as though personally called out.
> We should do our homework before playing.

### Scene 3 — Sharing, Talking, Stealing

**Panel 1** — Marwa standing shyly, hands fidgeting, small smile as she speaks.
> "I am proud that I share my food with my little brother."

**Panel 2** — Safiya nodding warmly and writing "We should share" on the board.
> "Good. Very good."

**Panel 3** — Noura standing tall and proud; the whole circle of students bursting into laughter around her.
> "I am proud that yesterday I did not talk too much."

**Panel 4** — Thuraya and John exchanging an amused look as Noura sits back down grinning.
> "That is a very big achievement." "You should do that again tomorrow."

**Panel 5** — Mansoor standing reluctantly, scratching his head before admitting his line; the class breaking into applause.
> "I am proud that I did not steal today."

**Panel 6** — John smiling, writing "We should be honest" on the chalkboard.
> "That is progress."

**Panel 7** — Khalil standing, delivering his line with a straight face; Thuraya standing with arms crossed, satisfied but firm.
> "I am proud that I did not put any bucket today." "Good. Keep it like that."

**Panel 8** — Safiya stepping forward to the board, chalking a list — "We shouldn't hurt others. We shouldn't lie. We shouldn't laugh at people." — students repeating in unison.
> They repeated together.

**Panel 9** — Close on the circle, everyone calm and orderly — then a small green shape darts into frame from off-panel, unnoticed by most.
> That is when something green jumped.

### Scene 4 — The Grasshopper

**Panel 1** — A large green grasshopper mid-flight, arcing through the air above the seated circle.
> A big green insect flew.

**Panel 2** — The grasshopper landing squarely on top of Safiya's head; everyone frozen for a beat, wide-eyed.
> For half a second, nobody moved.

**Panel 3** — Safiya on her feet, arms flailing wildly, mouth wide open mid-scream.
> "Aaaaaaaaah! Something is ALIVE on my head!"

**Panel 4** — Thuraya rushing toward her with hands raised, shouting not to move, while Safiya bolts in the opposite direction; students collapsing into laughter.
> "Don't move! Don't move!" — Safiya ran anyway.

**Panel 5** — The grasshopper now on the mat; Ali pointing at it excitedly, shouting to the group.
> "Jaradah! Grasshopper!"

**Panel 6** — Safiya staring down at the insect in horror as if it were alien; John standing nearby, forcing a brave face.
> "It is huge!" "It is just... an insect."

**Panel 7** — The grasshopper jumping again; Safiya recoiling and ducking behind Thuraya dramatically, hand to her forehead.
> "I should not come to Oman. I should live in a fridge."

**Panel 8** — Students doubled over, clutching their stomachs with laughter at the whole scene.
> The students were laughing so hard.

**Panel 9** — A long shadow falls across the group; Shayb Khalaf's thin, quiet silhouette stands over them. Children shrinking back, gasping.
> A familiar, thin, quiet figure appeared.

**Panel 10** — Shayb Khalaf calmly bending down to pluck the grasshopper off the ground bare-handed, then holding up a burlap bag full of live grasshoppers.
> "Who wants mashawi?"

**Panel 11** — Dead silence; Safiya's face drained white, hand halfway to her mouth, staring at the bag; Thuraya nodding beside her.
> "You eat that?" "Yes. People grill them."

**Panel 12** — Shayb Khalaf walking away unhurried, bag over his shoulder; the class left sitting in stunned silence behind him.
> Shayb Khalaf walked away. Like nothing happened.

### Scene 5 — The Wing

**Panel 1** — Interior, John and Safiya's simple village house at evening; Safiya seated at the table, still visibly shaken, gesturing as she talks.
> "I cannot believe people eat grasshoppers."

**Panel 2** — John across the table, unusually quiet, eating without meeting her eyes; Thuraya setting down a dish of food nearby.
> John was very quiet. Too quiet.

**Panel 3** — Safiya glancing over at John mid-bite, then pausing, leaning in slightly with narrowed eyes.
> She leaned closer. "...John."

**Panel 4** — Close-up: Safiya pointing directly at the corner of John's mouth, John looking back at her with feigned innocence.
> "John, why is there a wing... here?"

**Panel 5** — John freezing, slowly wiping his mouth with a napkin, then staring down at the napkin — a small insect wing visible on it.
> He looked at the napkin. It was a wing.

**Panel 6** — Safiya's eyes wide with shock, pointing accusingly; John holding up his hands in denial.
> "You ate it." "No."

**Panel 7** — Safiya standing with arms crossed, unimpressed; John deflating, admitting defeat with a sigh.
> "John." "...Yes."

**Panel 8** — Safiya recoiling toward the kitchen sink with a yelp; John leaning back in his chair, grinning proudly.
> "You ate mashawi grasshopper!" "Actually... not bad."

### Scene 6 — A Cup, a Smile, a Lesson Done

**Panel 1** — Safiya calling from the kitchen doorway, exasperated; John still seated at the table, laughing back at her.
> "You should see a doctor!" "You should try it!"

**Panel 2** — Safiya gripping a teacup tightly, arm half-raised as if to throw it, glaring at John.
> She almost threw a cup at him.

**Panel 3** — Close on the cup in her hand — one of a matching pair brought from England — her expression shifting from anger to reluctant restraint.
> The cup was one of two matching cups from England.

**Panel 4** — Safiya setting the cup down carefully on the counter, still glaring at John across the room.
> She put the cup down. Carefully.

**Panel 5** — Safiya's glare softening into a small, reluctant smile as she crosses back toward the table.
> "Okay. Tell me. Honestly. Was it bad?"

**Panel 6** — John leaning back, considering thoughtfully, describing the taste with his hands.
> "It was salty. Like fish. But... crunchy."

**Panel 7** — Safiya scrunching her face in disgust, shaking her head.
> "I am not ready."

**Panel 8** — Thuraya standing in the doorway, having watched the whole exchange, smiling with quiet satisfaction.
> "Today you learned should and shouldn't — and never refuse food in Oman."

**Panel 9** — John alone at a small desk before bed, writing in his notebook by lamplight.
> I should be brave with food. I should not lie to my wife.

**Panel 10** — Exterior night shot: quiet moonlit alleys of Samail, a lone goat standing in shadow.
> Somewhere in the alleys, a goat — almost certainly Malood — bleated once.


---


## Palm Thirty — Good Manners, Black Cats, and a Very Full Stomach

### Scene 1 — The Folded Paper

**Panel 1** — A village school classroom, morning light through windows. Ms. Thuraya enters holding a small folded paper, smiling in a distinctive, meaningful way. Children at their desks look up, sensing something is coming.
> "Today, we are invited to lunch."

**Panel 2** — Close on the students' faces, eyes wide with excitement.
> The students' eyes became big immediately.

**Panel 3** — Mansoor half-standing at his desk, asking eagerly. Thuraya facing the class.
> "Where?" — "To the house of Al-Mu'allim Hamad, the village Quran teacher."

**Panel 4** — Small inset/memory image: children sitting in a morning circle around an elderly white-bearded man (Al-Mu'allim Hamad), white kufiyyas on their heads, wooden tablets in laps, reciting Quran verses.
> A man who taught the Quran from morning prayer until late afternoon.

**Panel 5** — Safiya standing, clapping her hands with delight in front of the class.
> "Oh! A real Omani lunch!"

**Panel 6** — John off to the side, smiling politely but with a comic gleam of hunger in his eyes, hand near his stomach.
> John smiled politely. But his stomach smiled more.

**Panel 7** — Safiya standing at the chalkboard, writing the word "ETIQUETTE" in large letters.
> "Today is not only about food. Today is about etiquette."

**Panel 8** — John beside the board, adding a bulleted list beneath Safiya's word.
> Wash hands • Sit properly • Say please/thank you • Don't eat like Mansoor

**Panel 9** — Mansoor indignant, hands raised in protest, while Ali leans over whispering to a nearby student with a smirk.
> "I eat normally!" — Ali whispered, "Like a camel."

**Panel 10** — Whole class standing together, reciting in unison, Safiya and Thuraya nodding approvingly at the front.
> "We should be polite guests." Thuraya nodded. "Very important."

### Scene 2 — The Black Cat

**Panel 1** — Village lane at midday, soft sun, distant smoke rising from kitchen chimneys. Two groups walking: Thuraya, Safiya, and Fatkat with the girls behind; John and the boys ahead.
> The village was quiet. The smell of bread came from somewhere far.

**Panel 2** — A black cat crossing the dusty lane in front of the boys' group.
> Suddenly, something black crossed the road.

**Panel 3** — The boys frozen mid-step, some stepping backward, wary expressions.
> The boys stopped. Some stepped back.

**Panel 4** — Close on Mansoor, Khalil, and Ali whispering nervously to each other, glancing at the cat.
> "Sennoor aswad…" "Bad luck…" "Maybe sorcery…"

**Panel 5** — John laughing, gesturing casually at the cat while the boys watch him, unconvinced.
> "In Britain, this is just… a cat."

**Panel 6** — The black cat sitting regally, staring at the group, then turning and walking off with dignity.
> The black cat sat. Looked at them. Walked away. Like a king.

**Panel 7** — John grinning, joking to the boys.
> "Maybe this is Shayb Khalaf."

**Panel 8** — Sudden reveal: an elderly man (Shayb Khalaf) standing right behind John, having appeared silently. John and boys startled, jumping.
> "Why you say that, John?" Everyone jumped.

**Panel 9** — Close two-shot: Shayb Khalaf looking calmly at a pale, stammering John.
> "I look like a cat now?" John's face turned white. "No, no, no…"

**Panel 10** — Shayb Khalaf giving a small strange smile before turning to walk away slowly.
> "Good. Because cat don't fear."

**Panel 11** — Wide shot: John alone, looking around confused; the boys already far ahead down the lane.
> "Where did everyone go?" The boys were ten meters away.

**Panel 12** — John running to catch up to the laughing boys, arms flailing in mock protest.
> "You left me with him!"

### Scene 3 — Safiya and the Lizard

**Panel 1** — A sunlit village wall; Safiya, Thuraya, Fatkat, and the girls walking alongside it.
> Meanwhile, the girls were walking with Safiya, Thuraya, and Fatkat.
